package animlab

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"

	"gopkg.in/yaml.v3"
)

// Config represents the top-level YAML configuration for the SVG Animation Test Lab.
type Config struct {
	Version      int                    `yaml:"version" json:"version"`
	Inherits     string                 `yaml:"inherits,omitempty" json:"inherits,omitempty"`
	Presets      map[string]EffectSpec  `yaml:"presets,omitempty" json:"presets,omitempty"`
	Test         TestMeta               `yaml:"test" json:"test"`
	Browser      BrowserConfig          `yaml:"browser" json:"browser"`
	Execution    ExecutionConfig        `yaml:"execution" json:"execution"`
	Instances    InstancesConfig        `yaml:"instances" json:"instances"`
	Effects      []EffectSpec           `yaml:"effects" json:"effects"`
	Mixture      *MixtureConfig         `yaml:"mixture,omitempty" json:"mixture,omitempty"`
	Workloads    []WorkloadSpec         `yaml:"workloads" json:"workloads"`
	Measurements []string               `yaml:"measurements" json:"measurements"`
	Output       OutputConfig           `yaml:"output" json:"output"`
}

type TestMeta struct {
	Name        string `yaml:"name" json:"name"`
	Description string `yaml:"description" json:"description"`
}

type BrowserConfig struct {
	Engine         string `yaml:"engine" json:"engine"`
	Headless       bool   `yaml:"headless" json:"headless"`
	CDPEndpoint    string `yaml:"cdp_endpoint,omitempty" json:"cdp_endpoint,omitempty"`
	HostBindIP     string `yaml:"host_bind_ip,omitempty" json:"host_bind_ip,omitempty"`
	ProcessCPUHost string `yaml:"process_cpu_host,omitempty" json:"process_cpu_host,omitempty"`
	ViewportWidth  int    `yaml:"viewport_width,omitempty" json:"viewport_width,omitempty"`
	ViewportHeight int    `yaml:"viewport_height,omitempty" json:"viewport_height,omitempty"`
}

type ExecutionConfig struct {
	WarmupSeconds        float64 `yaml:"warmup_seconds" json:"warmup_seconds"`
	DurationSeconds      float64 `yaml:"duration_seconds" json:"duration_seconds"`
	Repetitions          int     `yaml:"repetitions" json:"repetitions"`
	RandomSeed           int64   `yaml:"random_seed" json:"random_seed"`
	AlternateMethodOrder bool    `yaml:"alternate_method_order" json:"alternate_method_order"`
}

type InstancesConfig struct {
	Counts    []int  `yaml:"counts" json:"counts"`
	Isolation string `yaml:"isolation" json:"isolation"` // "independent-svg-roots" | "shared-scene"
}

type EffectSpec struct {
	Preset          string             `yaml:"preset,omitempty" json:"preset,omitempty"`
	Family          string             `yaml:"family" json:"family"`
	Methods         []string           `yaml:"methods" json:"methods"`
	Shapes          []string           `yaml:"shapes,omitempty" json:"shapes,omitempty"`
	Sizes           map[string][2]int  `yaml:"sizes,omitempty" json:"sizes,omitempty"`
	Speeds          map[string]float64 `yaml:"speeds,omitempty" json:"speeds,omitempty"`
	SegmentCounts   []int              `yaml:"segment_counts,omitempty" json:"segment_counts,omitempty"`
	LineGapRatios   []string           `yaml:"line_gap_ratios,omitempty" json:"line_gap_ratios,omitempty"`
	PulseModes      []string           `yaml:"pulse_modes,omitempty" json:"pulse_modes,omitempty"`           // opacity-only | scale-only | scale-opacity
	PulseAmplitudes []float64          `yaml:"pulse_amplitudes,omitempty" json:"pulse_amplitudes,omitempty"` // e.g. [0.2, 0.35]
	FlashModes      []string           `yaml:"flash_modes,omitempty" json:"flash_modes,omitempty"`           // single-shot | repeating | concurrent | staggered
	FlashDurationMs int                `yaml:"flash_duration_ms,omitempty" json:"flash_duration_ms,omitempty"`
	FlashIntervalS  float64            `yaml:"flash_interval_s,omitempty" json:"flash_interval_s,omitempty"`
}

type MixtureConfig struct {
	Name    string             `yaml:"name,omitempty" json:"name,omitempty"`
	Methods []string           `yaml:"methods" json:"methods"`
	Weights map[string]float64 `yaml:"weights" json:"weights"`
}

type WorkloadSpec struct {
	Name             string `yaml:"name" json:"name"`
	Visibility       string `yaml:"visibility,omitempty" json:"visibility,omitempty"` // "all" | "dashboard"
	VisibleCount     int    `yaml:"visible_count,omitempty" json:"visible_count,omitempty"`
	SuspendOffscreen bool   `yaml:"suspend_offscreen,omitempty" json:"suspend_offscreen,omitempty"`
}

type OutputConfig struct {
	Dir            string `yaml:"dir,omitempty" json:"dir,omitempty"`
	Samples        bool   `yaml:"samples" json:"samples"`
	Screenshots    bool   `yaml:"screenshots" json:"screenshots"`
	RawResults     bool   `yaml:"raw_results" json:"raw_results"`
	HTMLReport     bool   `yaml:"html_report" json:"html_report"`
	MarkdownReport bool   `yaml:"markdown_report" json:"markdown_report"`
	DecisionReport bool   `yaml:"decision_report" json:"decision_report"`
}

var ValidMeasurements = map[string]bool{
	"process_cpu":           true,
	"frame_timing":          true,
	"long_tasks":            true,
	"rendering_activity":    true,
	"memory":                true,
	"animation_correctness": true,
}

// LoadConfig reads, resolves inheritance/presets, applies defaults, and validates a YAML config.
func LoadConfig(path string) (*Config, error) {
	cfg, err := loadConfigRaw(path, 0)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	applyDefaults(cfg)
	if err := resolvePresets(cfg); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if err := ValidateConfig(cfg); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return cfg, nil
}

func loadConfigRaw(path string, depth int) (*Config, error) {
	if depth > 5 {
		return nil, errors.New("YAML inheritance depth exceeded (possible cycle)")
	}
	raw, err := os.ReadFile(path)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading config %s: %w", path, err)
	}
	var cfg Config
	if err := yaml.Unmarshal(raw, &cfg); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parsing YAML %s: %w", path, err)
	}
	if cfg.Inherits != "" {
		basePath := cfg.Inherits
		if !filepath.IsAbs(basePath) {
			basePath = filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(path), basePath)
		}
		baseCfg, err := loadConfigRaw(basePath, depth+1)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("loading inherited base %s: %w", basePath, err)
		}
		cfg = mergeConfigs(*baseCfg, cfg)
	}
	return &cfg, nil
}

func mergeConfigs(base, over Config) Config {
	out := base
	if over.Version != 0 {
		out.Version = over.Version
	}
	out.Inherits = over.Inherits
	if len(over.Presets) > 0 {
		if out.Presets == nil {
			out.Presets = map[string]EffectSpec{}
		}
		for k, v := range over.Presets {
			out.Presets[k] = v
		}
	}
	if over.Test.Name != "" {
		out.Test.Name = over.Test.Name
	}
	if over.Test.Description != "" {
		out.Test.Description = over.Test.Description
	}
	if over.Browser.Engine != "" {
		out.Browser.Engine = over.Browser.Engine
	}
	if over.Browser.CDPEndpoint != "" {
		out.Browser.CDPEndpoint = over.Browser.CDPEndpoint
	}
	if over.Browser.HostBindIP != "" {
		out.Browser.HostBindIP = over.Browser.HostBindIP
	}
	if over.Browser.ProcessCPUHost != "" {
		out.Browser.ProcessCPUHost = over.Browser.ProcessCPUHost
	}
	if over.Browser.ViewportWidth > 0 {
		out.Browser.ViewportWidth = over.Browser.ViewportWidth
	}
	if over.Browser.ViewportHeight > 0 {
		out.Browser.ViewportHeight = over.Browser.ViewportHeight
	}
	if over.Execution.WarmupSeconds > 0 {
		out.Execution.WarmupSeconds = over.Execution.WarmupSeconds
	}
	if over.Execution.DurationSeconds > 0 {
		out.Execution.DurationSeconds = over.Execution.DurationSeconds
	}
	if over.Execution.Repetitions > 0 {
		out.Execution.Repetitions = over.Execution.Repetitions
	}
	if over.Execution.RandomSeed != 0 {
		out.Execution.RandomSeed = over.Execution.RandomSeed
	}
	if over.Execution.AlternateMethodOrder {
		out.Execution.AlternateMethodOrder = true
	}
	if len(over.Instances.Counts) > 0 {
		out.Instances.Counts = over.Instances.Counts
	}
	if over.Instances.Isolation != "" {
		out.Instances.Isolation = over.Instances.Isolation
	}
	if len(over.Effects) > 0 {
		out.Effects = over.Effects
	}
	if over.Mixture != nil {
		out.Mixture = over.Mixture
	}
	if len(over.Workloads) > 0 {
		out.Workloads = over.Workloads
	}
	if len(over.Measurements) > 0 {
		out.Measurements = over.Measurements
	}
	if over.Output != (OutputConfig{}) {
		out.Output = over.Output
	}
	return out
}

func applyDefaults(cfg *Config) {
	if cfg.Version == 0 {
		cfg.Version = 1
	}
	if cfg.Browser.Engine == "" {
		cfg.Browser.Engine = "chromium"
	}
	if cfg.Browser.CDPEndpoint == "" {
		cfg.Browser.CDPEndpoint = "http://127.0.0.1:9222"
	}
	if cfg.Browser.ViewportWidth <= 0 {
		cfg.Browser.ViewportWidth = 1366
	}
	if cfg.Browser.ViewportHeight <= 0 {
		cfg.Browser.ViewportHeight = 900
	}
	if cfg.Execution.DurationSeconds <= 0 {
		cfg.Execution.DurationSeconds = 5
	}
	if cfg.Execution.Repetitions <= 0 {
		cfg.Execution.Repetitions = 1
	}
	if cfg.Execution.RandomSeed == 0 {
		cfg.Execution.RandomSeed = 12345
	}
	if cfg.Instances.Isolation == "" {
		cfg.Instances.Isolation = "independent-svg-roots"
	}
	if len(cfg.Workloads) == 0 {
		cfg.Workloads = []WorkloadSpec{{Name: "all-active", Visibility: "all"}}
	}
	if len(cfg.Measurements) == 0 {
		cfg.Measurements = []string{"process_cpu", "frame_timing", "long_tasks", "rendering_activity", "memory", "animation_correctness"}
	}
	for i := range cfg.Effects {
		e := &cfg.Effects[i]
		if len(e.Shapes) == 0 {
			e.Shapes = defaultShapesForFamily(e.Family)
		}
		if len(e.Sizes) == 0 {
			e.Sizes = map[string][2]int{"medium": {120, 80}}
		}
		if len(e.Speeds) == 0 {
			e.Speeds = map[string]float64{"medium": 3.0}
		}
		if e.Family == "perimeter-tracer" {
			if len(e.SegmentCounts) == 0 {
				e.SegmentCounts = []int{2}
			}
			if len(e.LineGapRatios) == 0 {
				e.LineGapRatios = []string{"1:3"}
			}
		}
		if e.Family == "pulse" {
			if len(e.PulseModes) == 0 {
				e.PulseModes = []string{"scale-opacity"}
			}
			if len(e.PulseAmplitudes) == 0 {
				e.PulseAmplitudes = []float64{0.2}
			}
		}
		if e.Family == "flash" {
			if len(e.FlashModes) == 0 {
				e.FlashModes = []string{"repeating"}
			}
			if e.FlashDurationMs <= 0 {
				e.FlashDurationMs = 120
			}
			if e.FlashIntervalS <= 0 {
				e.FlashIntervalS = 1.2
			}
		}
	}
}

func defaultShapesForFamily(fam string) []string {
	switch fam {
	case "perimeter-tracer":
		return []string{"rectangle", "rounded-rectangle", "circle", "ellipse"}
	case "pulse":
		return []string{"circle", "ellipse", "rectangle", "diamond"}
	case "flash":
		return []string{"diamond", "cross", "star"}
	case "flow":
		return []string{"straight", "curved"}
	default:
		return []string{"circle"}
	}
}

func resolvePresets(cfg *Config) error {
	for i := range cfg.Effects {
		e := &cfg.Effects[i]
		if e.Preset == "" {
			continue
		}
		p, ok := cfg.Presets[e.Preset]
		if !ok {
			return fmt.Errorf("effect[%d] references unknown preset %q", i, e.Preset)
		}
		if e.Family == "" {
			e.Family = p.Family
		}
		if len(e.Methods) == 0 {
			e.Methods = append([]string(nil), p.Methods...)
		}
		if len(e.Shapes) == 0 {
			e.Shapes = append([]string(nil), p.Shapes...)
		}
		if len(e.Sizes) == 0 && len(p.Sizes) > 0 {
			e.Sizes = map[string][2]int{}
			for k, v := range p.Sizes {
				e.Sizes[k] = v
			}
		}
		if len(e.Speeds) == 0 && len(p.Speeds) > 0 {
			e.Speeds = map[string]float64{}
			for k, v := range p.Speeds {
				e.Speeds[k] = v
			}
		}
		if len(e.SegmentCounts) == 0 {
			e.SegmentCounts = append([]int(nil), p.SegmentCounts...)
		}
		if len(e.LineGapRatios) == 0 {
			e.LineGapRatios = append([]string(nil), p.LineGapRatios...)
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// ValidateConfig checks schema invariants and returns clear error messages.
func ValidateConfig(cfg *Config) error {
	if cfg == nil {
		return errors.New("nil configuration")
	}
	if cfg.Version != 1 {
		return fmt.Errorf("unsupported config version %d (expected 1)", cfg.Version)
	}
	if strings.TrimSpace(cfg.Test.Name) == "" {
		return errors.New("test.name is required")
	}
	if strings.ToLower(cfg.Browser.Engine) != "chromium" {
		return fmt.Errorf("unsupported browser.engine %q (supported: chromium)", cfg.Browser.Engine)
	}
	if cfg.Execution.WarmupSeconds < 0 {
		return errors.New("execution.warmup_seconds cannot be negative")
	}
	if cfg.Execution.DurationSeconds <= 0 {
		return errors.New("execution.duration_seconds must be > 0")
	}
	if cfg.Execution.Repetitions < 1 {
		return errors.New("execution.repetitions must be >= 1")
	}
	if len(cfg.Instances.Counts) == 0 {
		return errors.New("instances.counts must specify at least one positive integer count")
	}
	for _, c := range cfg.Instances.Counts {
		if c <= 0 {
			return fmt.Errorf("instances.counts entries must be positive integers, got %d", c)
		}
	}
	if cfg.Instances.Isolation != "independent-svg-roots" && cfg.Instances.Isolation != "shared-scene" {
		return fmt.Errorf("instances.isolation must be 'independent-svg-roots' or 'shared-scene', got %q", cfg.Instances.Isolation)
	}
	if len(cfg.Effects) == 0 && cfg.Mixture == nil {
		return errors.New("at least one entry in effects or a mixture section is required")
	}

	reg := DefaultRegistry()
	for i, e := range cfg.Effects {
		gen, ok := reg[e.Family]
		if !ok {
			return fmt.Errorf("effects[%d]: unknown family %q", i, e.Family)
		}
		if len(e.Methods) == 0 {
			return fmt.Errorf("effects[%d] (%s): at least one rendering method is required", i, e.Family)
		}
		for _, m := range e.Methods {
			if !gen.SupportsMethod(m) {
				return fmt.Errorf("effects[%d] (%s): unsupported method %q (supported: %v)", i, e.Family, m, gen.SupportedMethods())
			}
		}
		for szName, dims := range e.Sizes {
			if dims[0] <= 0 || dims[1] <= 0 {
				return fmt.Errorf("effects[%d].sizes[%s]: width and height must be positive, got %v", i, szName, dims)
			}
		}
		for spName, sec := range e.Speeds {
			if sec <= 0 {
				return fmt.Errorf("effects[%d].speeds[%s]: duration must be > 0, got %v", i, spName, sec)
			}
		}
	}

	if cfg.Mixture != nil {
		if len(cfg.Mixture.Weights) == 0 {
			return errors.New("mixture.weights must specify at least one effect family weight")
		}
		totalW := 0.0
		for fam, w := range cfg.Mixture.Weights {
			if _, ok := reg[fam]; !ok {
				return fmt.Errorf("mixture.weights: unknown effect family %q", fam)
			}
			if w <= 0 {
				return fmt.Errorf("mixture.weights[%s]: weight must be > 0, got %v", fam, w)
			}
			totalW += w
		}
		if totalW <= 0 {
			return errors.New("mixture.weights total must be > 0")
		}
		if len(cfg.Mixture.Methods) == 0 {
			return errors.New("mixture.methods must specify at least one method")
		}
	}

	for i, wl := range cfg.Workloads {
		if strings.TrimSpace(wl.Name) == "" {
			return fmt.Errorf("workloads[%d].name is required", i)
		}
		if wl.Visibility != "" && wl.Visibility != "all" && wl.Visibility != "dashboard" {
			return fmt.Errorf("workloads[%d].visibility must be 'all' or 'dashboard', got %q", i, wl.Visibility)
		}
	}

	for _, m := range cfg.Measurements {
		if !ValidMeasurements[m] {
			return fmt.Errorf("unknown measurement %q", m)
		}
	}
	return nil
}


========== FILE: pkg/animlab/config_test.go ==========
package animlab

import (
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

func TestYAMLInheritancePresetsAndValidation(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	baseYAML := `version: 1
presets:
  tracer_std:
    family: perimeter-tracer
    methods: [css, smil]
    shapes: [rectangle, circle]
    sizes:
      small: [40, 40]
    speeds:
      fast: 0.8
    segment_counts: [2]
    line_gap_ratios: ["1:3"]
test:
  name: base-test
  description: Base
browser:
  engine: chromium
  headless: true
execution:
  warmup_seconds: 0.5
  duration_seconds: 1.0
  repetitions: 1
  random_seed: 42
instances:
  counts: [50]
  isolation: independent-svg-roots
effects:
  - preset: tracer_std
workloads:
  - name: all-active
    visibility: all
`
	childYAML := `inherits: base.yaml
test:
  name: child-test
instances:
  counts: [100, 250]
  isolation: independent-svg-roots
`
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "base.yaml"), []byte(baseYAML), 0644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	childPath := filepath.Join(dir, "child.yaml")
	if err := os.WriteFile(childPath, []byte(childYAML), 0644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	cfg, err := LoadConfig(childPath)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("LoadConfig failed: %v", err)
	}
	if cfg.Test.Name != "child-test" {
		t.Fatalf("expected child-test, got %q", cfg.Test.Name)
	}
	if len(cfg.Instances.Counts) != 2 || cfg.Instances.Counts[0] != 100 || cfg.Instances.Counts[1] != 250 {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected counts: %v", cfg.Instances.Counts)
	}
	specs := ExpandScenarios(cfg)
	if len(specs) != 4 { // 2 counts * 2 methods (css, smil)
		t.Fatalf("expected 4 scenarios, got %d", len(specs))
	}
	if specs[0].Count != 100 || specs[2].Count != 250 {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected scenario counts: %d, %d", specs[0].Count, specs[2].Count)
	}
	html := BuildTestPageHTML(specs[0])
	if !strings.Contains(html, `class="lab-svg-root"`) {
		t.Fatalf("expected independent SVG roots in HTML")
	}

	// Invalid config check
	badPath := filepath.Join(dir, "bad.yaml")
	_ = os.WriteFile(badPath, []byte("version: 1\ntest:\n  name: bad\ninstances:\n  counts: [0]\neffects:\n  - family: unknown\n    methods: [css]\n"), 0644)
	if _, err := LoadConfig(badPath); err == nil {
		t.Fatalf("expected validation error for invalid config")
	}
}

func TestMixedAndSharedSceneWorkloads(t *testing.T) {
	cfg := &Config{
		Version: 1,
		Test:    TestMeta{Name: "mix"},
		Browser: BrowserConfig{Engine: "chromium"},
		Execution: ExecutionConfig{
			DurationSeconds: 1,
			Repetitions:     1,
			RandomSeed:      99,
		},
		Instances: InstancesConfig{
			Counts:    []int{50},
			Isolation: "shared-scene",
		},
		Mixture: &MixtureConfig{
			Name:    "mixed-dash",
			Methods: []string{"css", "smil"},
			Weights: map[string]float64{
				"perimeter-tracer": 35,
				"pulse":            25,
				"flow":             20,
				"static-gauge":     20,
			},
		},
		Workloads: []WorkloadSpec{{Name: "all-active", Visibility: "all"}},
	}
	applyDefaults(cfg)
	if err := ValidateConfig(cfg); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	specs := ExpandScenarios(cfg)
	if len(specs) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 2 mixed scenarios, got %d", len(specs))
	}
	html := BuildTestPageHTML(specs[0])
	if !strings.Contains(html, `class="lab-shared-wrap"`) {
		t.Fatalf("expected shared-scene wrapper")
	}
}


========== FILE: pkg/animlab/effects.go ==========
package animlab

import (
	"fmt"
	"math"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
)

// InstanceParams defines the concrete authored parameters for a single widget/object instance.
type InstanceParams struct {
	ID             string
	Family         string
	Method         string
	Shape          string
	Width          int
	Height         int
	Speed          float64 // circuit or cycle duration in seconds
	Segments       int
	LineGapRatio   string
	Direction      string // "cw" | "ccw"
	PulseMode      string // "opacity-only" | "scale-only" | "scale-opacity"
	PulseAmplitude float64
	FlashMode      string // "single-shot" | "repeating" | "concurrent" | "staggered"
	FlashDurMs     int
	FlashIntervalS float64
	StaggerDelayS  float64
	Color          string
}

// RenderedEffect holds the generated CSS, SVG markup, optional JS driver, and any method limitation.
type RenderedEffect struct {
	CSS        string
	SVGInner   string
	JSDriver   string
	Limitation string
}

// EffectGenerator is the pluggable interface for adding new effect families and rendering methods.
type EffectGenerator interface {
	Family() string
	Description() string
	SupportedMethods() []string
	SupportsMethod(method string) bool
	Render(p InstanceParams, viewW, viewH int) RenderedEffect
}

// DefaultRegistry returns all registered effect families.
func DefaultRegistry() map[string]EffectGenerator {
	return map[string]EffectGenerator{
		"perimeter-tracer": tracerGen{},
		"pulse":            pulseGen{},
		"flash":            flashGen{},
		"flow":             flowGen{},
		"static-gauge":     gaugeGen{},
	}
}

func containsStr(list []string, target string) bool {
	for _, s := range list {
		if s == target {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

func computeDash(segments int, ratio string) (float64, float64) {
	if segments < 1 {
		segments = 1
	}
	l, g := 1.0, 3.0
	parts := strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(ratio), ":")
	if len(parts) == 2 {
		if lv, err := strconv.ParseFloat(strings.TrimSpace(parts[0]), 64); err == nil && lv > 0 {
			if gv, err2 := strconv.ParseFloat(strings.TrimSpace(parts[1]), 64); err2 == nil && gv > 0 {
				l, g = lv, gv
			}
		}
	}
	unit := 1000.0 / float64(segments)
	dash := math.Round((unit*(l/(l+g)))*1000) / 1000
	gap := math.Round((unit-dash)*1000) / 1000
	return dash, gap
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 1. Perimeter Tracer
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
type tracerGen struct{}

func (tracerGen) Family() string      { return "perimeter-tracer" }
func (tracerGen) Description() string { return "Illuminated segments travelling around closed shapes" }
func (tracerGen) SupportedMethods() []string {
	return []string{"css", "smil", "javascript-raf", "clip-reveal", "static"}
}
func (t tracerGen) SupportsMethod(m string) bool { return containsStr(t.SupportedMethods(), m) }

func (tracerGen) Render(p InstanceParams, viewW, viewH int) RenderedEffect {
	cx := float64(viewW) / 2.0
	cy := float64(viewH) / 2.0
	w := float64(p.Width)
	h := float64(p.Height)
	if w <= 0 {
		w = 120
	}
	if h <= 0 {
		h = 80
	}
	if p.Shape == "circle" {
		h = w
	}
	rx := w / 2.0
	ry := h / 2.0
	x := cx - rx
	y := cy - ry
	dur := math.Max(0.2, p.Speed)
	dash, gap := computeDash(p.Segments, p.LineGapRatio)
	dashAttr := fmt.Sprintf("%.3f %.3f", dash, gap)
	toOffset := "-1000"
	if p.Direction == "ccw" {
		toOffset = "1000"
	}

	buildShape := func(extra string) string {
		switch p.Shape {
		case "circle", "ellipse":
			return fmt.Sprintf(`<ellipse cx="%.2f" cy="%.2f" rx="%.2f" ry="%.2f" %s />`, cx, cy, rx, ry, extra)
		case "rounded-rectangle":
			cr := math.Min(10, math.Min(rx, ry))
			return fmt.Sprintf(`<rect x="%.2f" y="%.2f" width="%.2f" height="%.2f" rx="%.2f" %s />`, x, y, w, h, cr, extra)
		default:
			return fmt.Sprintf(`<rect x="%.2f" y="%.2f" width="%.2f" height="%.2f" %s />`, x, y, w, h, extra)
		}
	}

	guide := buildShape(fmt.Sprintf(`class="lab-guide" fill="none" stroke="%s" stroke-width="1.2" stroke-opacity="0.22" shape-rendering="geometricPrecision"`, p.Color))
	common := fmt.Sprintf(`class="lab-payload" data-lab-family="perimeter-tracer" data-lab-method="%s" data-lab-dir="%s" data-lab-dur="%.3f" pathLength="1000" fill="none" stroke="%s" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-dasharray="%s" stroke-linecap="round" shape-rendering="geometricPrecision"`,
		p.Method, p.Direction, dur, p.Color, dashAttr)

	switch p.Method {
	case "css":
		kf := p.ID + "_kf"
		css := fmt.Sprintf(`@keyframes %s { from { stroke-dashoffset: 0px; } to { stroke-dashoffset: %spx; } }
#%s .lab-payload { animation: %s %.3fs linear infinite; }`, kf, toOffset, p.ID, kf, dur)
		return RenderedEffect{CSS: css, SVGInner: guide + buildShape(common)}

	case "smil":
		anim := fmt.Sprintf(`<animate attributeName="stroke-dashoffset" from="0" to="%s" dur="%.3fs" repeatCount="indefinite" />`, toOffset, dur)
		shape := buildShape(common)
		shape = strings.TrimSuffix(shape, " />") + ">" + anim + "</" + shapeTagName(p.Shape) + ">"
		return RenderedEffect{SVGInner: guide + shape}

	case "javascript-raf":
		js := fmt.Sprintf(`window.__labRafDrivers.push({ id: %q, kind: 'tracer', dur: %.3f, dir: %q });`, p.ID, dur, p.Direction)
		return RenderedEffect{SVGInner: guide + buildShape(common), JSDriver: js}

	case "clip-reveal":
		clipID := p.ID + "_clip"
		kf := p.ID + "_clipkf"
		css := fmt.Sprintf(`@keyframes %s { 0%% { transform: rotate(0deg); } 100%% { transform: rotate(%sdeg); } }
#%s_sweep { transform-origin: %.2fpx %.2fpx; animation: %s %.3fs linear infinite; }`,
			kf, map[bool]string{true: "-360", false: "360"}[p.Direction == "ccw"], p.ID, cx, cy, kf, dur)
		clip := fmt.Sprintf(`<defs><clipPath id="%s"><path id="%s_sweep" d="M %.2f %.2f L %.2f %.2f A %.2f %.2f 0 0 1 %.2f %.2f Z"/></clipPath></defs>`,
			clipID, p.ID, cx, cy, cx, cy-math.Max(rx, ry)*1.6, math.Max(rx, ry)*1.6, math.Max(rx, ry)*1.6, cx+math.Max(rx, ry)*1.6, cy)
		pay := buildShape(fmt.Sprintf(`class="lab-payload" data-lab-family="perimeter-tracer" data-lab-method="clip-reveal" fill="none" stroke="%s" stroke-width="2.5" clip-path="url(#%s)" shape-rendering="geometricPrecision"`, p.Color, clipID))
		return RenderedEffect{
			CSS:        css,
			SVGInner:   clip + guide + pay,
			Limitation: "clip-reveal sweeps a single angular wedge and cannot independently space N segments at arbitrary line:gap ratios",
		}

	default: // static
		return RenderedEffect{SVGInner: guide + buildShape(common)}
	}
}

func shapeTagName(shape string) string {
	if shape == "circle" || shape == "ellipse" {
		return "ellipse"
	}
	return "rect"
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 2. Pulse
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
type pulseGen struct{}

func (pulseGen) Family() string      { return "pulse" }
func (pulseGen) Description() string { return "Breathing scale and/or opacity indicator" }
func (pulseGen) SupportedMethods() []string {
	return []string{"css", "smil", "javascript-raf", "static"}
}
func (p pulseGen) SupportsMethod(m string) bool { return containsStr(p.SupportedMethods(), m) }

func (pulseGen) Render(p InstanceParams, viewW, viewH int) RenderedEffect {
	cx := float64(viewW) / 2.0
	cy := float64(viewH) / 2.0
	w := math.Max(5, float64(p.Width))
	h := math.Max(5, float64(p.Height))
	if p.Shape == "circle" {
		h = w
	}
	rx, ry := w/2.0, h/2.0
	dur := math.Max(0.2, p.Speed)
	amp := p.PulseAmplitude
	if amp <= 0 {
		amp = 0.2
	}
	minS := math.Max(0.2, 1.0-amp)
	maxS := 1.0 + amp
	minOp := 0.35
	if p.PulseMode == "scale-only" {
		minOp = 0.95
	}
	if p.PulseMode == "opacity-only" {
		minS, maxS = 1.0, 1.0
	}

	buildShape := func(lx, ly float64, extra string) string {
		switch p.Shape {
		case "rectangle", "rounded-rectangle":
			return fmt.Sprintf(`<rect x="%.2f" y="%.2f" width="%.2f" height="%.2f" rx="3" %s />`, lx-rx, ly-ry, w, h, extra)
		case "diamond":
			pts := fmt.Sprintf("%.2f,%.2f %.2f,%.2f %.2f,%.2f %.2f,%.2f", lx, ly-ry, lx+rx, ly, lx, ly+ry, lx-rx, ly)
			return fmt.Sprintf(`<polygon points="%s" %s />`, pts, extra)
		default:
			return fmt.Sprintf(`<ellipse cx="%.2f" cy="%.2f" rx="%.2f" ry="%.2f" %s />`, lx, ly, rx, ry, extra)
		}
	}

	common := fmt.Sprintf(`class="lab-payload" data-lab-family="pulse" data-lab-method="%s" data-lab-mode="%s" data-lab-dur="%.3f" fill="%s" shape-rendering="geometricPrecision"`,
		p.Method, p.PulseMode, dur, p.Color)

	switch p.Method {
	case "css":
		kf := p.ID + "_pkf"
		css := fmt.Sprintf(`@keyframes %s { 0%%, 100%% { transform: scale(%.3f); opacity: %.3f; } 50%% { transform: scale(%.3f); opacity: 1; } }
#%s .lab-payload { transform-box: fill-box; transform-origin: center; will-change: transform, opacity; animation: %s %.3fs ease-in-out infinite; }`,
			kf, minS, minOp, maxS, p.ID, kf, dur)
		return RenderedEffect{CSS: css, SVGInner: buildShape(cx, cy, common)}

	case "smil":
		animT := fmt.Sprintf(`<animateTransform attributeName="transform" type="scale" values="%.3f;%.3f;%.3f" keyTimes="0;0.5;1" calcMode="spline" keySplines="0.42 0 0.58 1;0.42 0 0.58 1" dur="%.3fs" repeatCount="indefinite" />`,
			minS, maxS, minS, dur)
		animO := fmt.Sprintf(`<animate attributeName="opacity" values="%.3f;1;%.3f" keyTimes="0;0.5;1" calcMode="spline" keySplines="0.42 0 0.58 1;0.42 0 0.58 1" dur="%.3fs" repeatCount="indefinite" />`,
			minOp, minOp, dur)
		inner := buildShape(0, 0, common)
		tag := "ellipse"
		if p.Shape == "rectangle" || p.Shape == "rounded-rectangle" {
			tag = "rect"
		} else if p.Shape == "diamond" {
			tag = "polygon"
		}
		inner = strings.TrimSuffix(inner, " />") + ">" + animT + animO + "</" + tag + ">"
		return RenderedEffect{SVGInner: fmt.Sprintf(`<g transform="translate(%.2f,%.2f)">%s</g>`, cx, cy, inner)}

	case "javascript-raf":
		js := fmt.Sprintf(`window.__labRafDrivers.push({ id: %q, kind: 'pulse', dur: %.3f, minS: %.3f, maxS: %.3f, minOp: %.3f });`,
			p.ID, dur, minS, maxS, minOp)
		return RenderedEffect{SVGInner: fmt.Sprintf(`<g transform="translate(%.2f,%.2f)">%s</g>`, cx, cy, buildShape(0, 0, common)), JSDriver: js}

	default:
		return RenderedEffect{SVGInner: buildShape(cx, cy, common)}
	}
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 3. Flash
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
type flashGen struct{}

func (flashGen) Family() string      { return "flash" }
func (flashGen) Description() string { return "Brief event flash with single-shot, repeating, or staggered timing" }
func (flashGen) SupportedMethods() []string {
	return []string{"css", "smil", "javascript-raf", "static"}
}
func (f flashGen) SupportsMethod(m string) bool { return containsStr(f.SupportedMethods(), m) }

func (flashGen) Render(p InstanceParams, viewW, viewH int) RenderedEffect {
	cx, cy := float64(viewW)/2.0, float64(viewH)/2.0
	w := math.Max(5, float64(p.Width))
	h := math.Max(5, float64(p.Height))
	rx, ry := w/2.0, h/2.0
	durS := math.Max(0.03, float64(p.FlashDurMs)/1000.0)
	if durS <= 0 {
		durS = 0.12
	}
	ivS := math.Max(durS+0.05, p.FlashIntervalS)
	if ivS <= 0 {
		ivS = 1.2
	}

	buildShape := func(extra string) string {
		if p.Shape == "cross" {
			ha := math.Max(0.5, math.Min(math.Min(rx*0.9, ry*0.9), 1.5))
			d := fmt.Sprintf("M %.2f,%.2f L %.2f,%.2f L %.2f,%.2f L %.2f,%.2f L %.2f,%.2f L %.2f,%.2f L %.2f,%.2f L %.2f,%.2f L %.2f,%.2f L %.2f,%.2f L %.2f,%.2f L %.2f,%.2f Z",
				cx-ha, cy-ry, cx+ha, cy-ry, cx+ha, cy-ha, cx+rx, cy-ha, cx+rx, cy+ha, cx+ha, cy+ha, cx+ha, cy+ry, cx-ha, cy+ry, cx-ha, cy+ha, cx-rx, cy+ha, cx-rx, cy-ha, cx-ha, cy-ha)
			return fmt.Sprintf(`<path d="%s" %s />`, d, extra)
		}
		pts := fmt.Sprintf("%.2f,%.2f %.2f,%.2f %.2f,%.2f %.2f,%.2f", cx, cy-ry, cx+rx, cy, cx, cy+ry, cx-rx, cy)
		return fmt.Sprintf(`<polygon points="%s" %s />`, pts, extra)
	}

	common := fmt.Sprintf(`class="lab-payload" data-lab-family="flash" data-lab-method="%s" data-lab-mode="%s" fill="%s" shape-rendering="geometricPrecision"`,
		p.Method, p.FlashMode, p.Color)

	switch p.Method {
	case "css":
		kf := p.ID + "_fkf"
		pct := math.Min(90, (durS/ivS)*100.0)
		iter := "infinite"
		totalDur := ivS
		if p.FlashMode == "single-shot" {
			iter = "1"
			totalDur = durS
			pct = 30
		}
		delay := p.StaggerDelayS
		css := fmt.Sprintf(`@keyframes %s { 0%% { opacity: 0; transform: scale(0.85); } %.1f%% { opacity: 0.95; transform: scale(1.08); } %.1f%%, 100%% { opacity: 0; transform: scale(1.0); } }
#%s .lab-payload { transform-box: fill-box; transform-origin: center; will-change: transform, opacity; animation: %s %.3fs ease-out %.3fs %s both; }`,
			kf, pct*0.25, pct, p.ID, kf, totalDur, delay, iter)
		return RenderedEffect{CSS: css, SVGInner: buildShape(common)}

	case "smil":
		totalDur := ivS
		rep := "indefinite"
		if p.FlashMode == "single-shot" {
			totalDur = durS
			rep = "1"
		}
		kt := math.Min(0.9, durS/totalDur)
		anim := fmt.Sprintf(`<animate attributeName="opacity" values="0;0.95;0;0" keyTimes="0;%.3f;%.3f;1" dur="%.3fs" begin="%.3fs" repeatCount="%s" fill="freeze" />`,
			kt*0.25, kt, totalDur, p.StaggerDelayS, rep)
		shape := buildShape(common)
		tag := "polygon"
		if p.Shape == "cross" {
			tag = "path"
		}
		shape = strings.TrimSuffix(shape, " />") + ">" + anim + "</" + tag + ">"
		return RenderedEffect{SVGInner: shape}

	case "javascript-raf":
		js := fmt.Sprintf(`window.__labRafDrivers.push({ id: %q, kind: 'flash', dur: %.3f, interval: %.3f, stagger: %.3f, single: %v });`,
			p.ID, durS, ivS, p.StaggerDelayS, p.FlashMode == "single-shot")
		return RenderedEffect{SVGInner: buildShape(common), JSDriver: js}

	default:
		return RenderedEffect{SVGInner: buildShape(common)}
	}
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 4. Flow (Existing Studio reference & simpler alternatives)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
type flowGen struct{}

func (flowGen) Family() string      { return "flow" }
func (flowGen) Description() string { return "Connector flow animation (Studio SMIL reference vs CSS/RAF line alternatives)" }
func (flowGen) SupportedMethods() []string {
	return []string{"smil", "css", "javascript-raf", "static"}
}
func (f flowGen) SupportsMethod(m string) bool { return containsStr(f.SupportedMethods(), m) }

func (flowGen) Render(p InstanceParams, viewW, viewH int) RenderedEffect {
	x1, y1 := 14.0, float64(viewH)*0.65
	x2, y2 := float64(viewW)-14.0, float64(viewH)*0.35
	pathD := fmt.Sprintf("M %.1f %.1f L %.1f %.1f", x1, y1, x2, y2)
	if p.Shape == "curved" {
		pathD = fmt.Sprintf("M %.1f %.1f Q %.1f %.1f %.1f %.1f", x1, y1, float64(viewW)/2, 8.0, x2, y2)
	}
	dur := math.Max(0.3, p.Speed)
	pathID := p.ID + "_path"

	switch p.Method {
	case "smil":
		// Existing Studio Flow pattern: dashed track animate + animateMotion payload
		track := fmt.Sprintf(`<path id="%s" d="%s" fill="none" stroke="%s" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-dasharray="10,6" stroke-linecap="round" opacity="0.85"><animate attributeName="stroke-dashoffset" from="32" to="0" dur="1s" repeatCount="indefinite" /></path>`,
			pathID, pathD, p.Color)
		particle := fmt.Sprintf(`<circle class="lab-payload" data-lab-family="flow" data-lab-method="smil" r="4.5" fill="%s"><animateMotion calcMode="linear" dur="%.3fs" repeatCount="indefinite" rotate="auto"><mpath href="#%s" /></animateMotion></circle>`,
			p.Color, dur, pathID)
		return RenderedEffect{SVGInner: track + particle}

	case "css":
		kf := p.ID + "_flowkf"
		css := fmt.Sprintf(`@keyframes %s { from { stroke-dashoffset: 0px; } to { stroke-dashoffset: -1000px; } }
#%s .lab-payload { animation: %s %.3fs linear infinite; }`, kf, p.ID, kf, dur)
		line := fmt.Sprintf(`<path class="lab-payload" data-lab-family="flow" data-lab-method="css" d="%s" pathLength="1000" fill="none" stroke="%s" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-dasharray="140 160" stroke-linecap="round" />`,
			pathD, p.Color)
		return RenderedEffect{CSS: css, SVGInner: line}

	case "javascript-raf":
		js := fmt.Sprintf(`window.__labRafDrivers.push({ id: %q, kind: 'tracer', dur: %.3f, dir: 'cw' });`, p.ID, dur)
		line := fmt.Sprintf(`<path class="lab-payload" data-lab-family="flow" data-lab-method="javascript-raf" d="%s" pathLength="1000" fill="none" stroke="%s" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-dasharray="140 160" stroke-linecap="round" />`,
			pathD, p.Color)
		return RenderedEffect{SVGInner: line, JSDriver: js}

	default:
		line := fmt.Sprintf(`<path class="lab-payload" data-lab-family="flow" data-lab-method="static" d="%s" fill="none" stroke="%s" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-dasharray="10,6" stroke-linecap="round" />`,
			pathD, p.Color)
		return RenderedEffect{SVGInner: line}
	}
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 5. Static Gauge (for mixed workloads & data-driven updates)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
type gaugeGen struct{}

func (gaugeGen) Family() string             { return "static-gauge" }
func (gaugeGen) Description() string        { return "Static or data-driven arc gauge for mixed dashboard workloads" }
func (gaugeGen) SupportedMethods() []string { return []string{"static", "data-driven", "css", "smil", "javascript-raf"} }
func (g gaugeGen) SupportsMethod(m string) bool { return containsStr(g.SupportedMethods(), m) }

func (gaugeGen) Render(p InstanceParams, viewW, viewH int) RenderedEffect {
	cx, cy := float64(viewW)/2.0, float64(viewH)/2.0
	r := math.Max(10, math.Min(float64(p.Width), float64(p.Height))/2.0-4)
	bg := fmt.Sprintf(`<circle cx="%.2f" cy="%.2f" r="%.2f" fill="none" stroke="#30363d" stroke-width="5" />`, cx, cy, r)
	arc := fmt.Sprintf(`<circle class="lab-payload" data-lab-family="static-gauge" data-lab-method="%s" cx="%.2f" cy="%.2f" r="%.2f" pathLength="100" fill="none" stroke="%s" stroke-width="5" stroke-dasharray="68 100" stroke-linecap="round" transform="rotate(-90 %.2f %.2f)" />`,
		p.Method, cx, cy, r, p.Color, cx, cy)
	return RenderedEffect{SVGInner: bg + arc}
}


========== FILE: pkg/animlab/report.go ==========
package animlab

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"html"
	"math"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sort"
	"strings"

	"gopkg.in/yaml.v3"
)

// WriteAllOutputs writes resolved-config.yaml, raw-results.json, Markdown/HTML reports,
// per-effect Decision Reports, and the Visual Demonstration Catalogue.
func WriteAllOutputs(cfg *Config, res *LabResults, outDir string) error {
	if err := os.MkdirAll(outDir, 0755); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	// 1. Always preserve exact resolved YAML configuration (self-contained, inheritance already resolved)
	resolvedCfg := *cfg
	resolvedCfg.Inherits = ""
	resolvedBytes, err := yaml.Marshal(&resolvedCfg)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(outDir, "resolved-config.yaml"), resolvedBytes, 0644); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	// 2. Raw machine-readable JSON results
	if cfg.Output.RawResults && res != nil {
		raw, err := json.MarshalIndent(res, "", "  ")
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(outDir, "raw-results.json"), raw, 0644); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}

	// 3. Visual Samples & Browsable Catalogue
	var sampleFiles []SampleEntry
	if cfg.Output.Samples {
		var err error
		sampleFiles, err = GenerateVisualCatalogue(cfg, outDir, res != nil)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}

	if res == nil {
		return nil
	}

	// 4. Markdown Report
	if cfg.Output.MarkdownReport {
		md := BuildMarkdownReport(cfg, res)
		if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(outDir, "report.md"), []byte(md), 0644); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}

	// 5. HTML Report
	if cfg.Output.HTMLReport {
		h := BuildHTMLReport(cfg, res, sampleFiles)
		if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(outDir, "report.html"), []byte(h), 0644); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}

	// 6. Per-Effect Decision Reports
	if cfg.Output.DecisionReport {
		if err := WriteDecisionReports(cfg, res, outDir); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}

	return nil
}

type SampleEntry struct {
	Filename   string
	Family     string
	Method     string
	Shape      string
	Size       string
	Speed      string
	Limitation string
}

// GenerateVisualCatalogue writes standalone SVG samples and a browsable HTML catalogue.
func GenerateVisualCatalogue(cfg *Config, outDir string, hasReport bool) ([]SampleEntry, error) {
	sampDir := filepath.Join(outDir, "samples")
	catDir := filepath.Join(outDir, "catalogue")
	if err := os.MkdirAll(sampDir, 0755); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if err := os.MkdirAll(catDir, 0755); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	reg := DefaultRegistry()
	var entries []SampleEntry
	idx := 0
	for _, eff := range cfg.Effects {
		gen := reg[eff.Family]
		if gen == nil {
			continue
		}
		sizeKeys := sortedMapKeys(eff.Sizes)
		speedKeys := sortedMapKeys(eff.Speeds)
		for _, m := range eff.Methods {
			for _, sh := range eff.Shapes {
				for _, szK := range sizeKeys {
					sz := eff.Sizes[szK]
					for _, spK := range speedKeys {
						idx++
						sp := eff.Speeds[spK]
						seg := 2
						if len(eff.SegmentCounts) > 0 {
							seg = eff.SegmentCounts[0]
						}
						ratio := "1:3"
						if len(eff.LineGapRatios) > 0 {
							ratio = eff.LineGapRatios[0]
						}
						pMode := "scale-opacity"
						if len(eff.PulseModes) > 0 {
							pMode = eff.PulseModes[0]
						}
						pAmp := 0.2
						if len(eff.PulseAmplitudes) > 0 {
							pAmp = eff.PulseAmplitudes[0]
						}
						fMode := "repeating"
						if len(eff.FlashModes) > 0 {
							fMode = eff.FlashModes[0]
						}
						ip := InstanceParams{
							ID:             fmt.Sprintf("samp_%d", idx),
							Family:         eff.Family,
							Method:         m,
							Shape:          sh,
							Width:          sz[0],
							Height:         sz[1],
							Speed:          sp,
							Segments:       seg,
							LineGapRatio:   ratio,
							Direction:      "cw",
							PulseMode:      pMode,
							PulseAmplitude: pAmp,
							FlashMode:      fMode,
							FlashDurMs:     eff.FlashDurationMs,
							FlashIntervalS: eff.FlashIntervalS,
							Color:          palette[(idx-1)%len(palette)],
						}
						re := gen.Render(ip, 220, 140)
						svg := BuildStandaloneSampleSVG(ip)
						fname := fmt.Sprintf("%s__%s__%s__%s__%s.svg", eff.Family, m, sh, szK, spK)
						if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(sampDir, fname), []byte(svg), 0644); err != nil {
							return nil, err
						}
						entries = append(entries, SampleEntry{
							Filename:   fname,
							Family:     eff.Family,
							Method:     m,
							Shape:      sh,
							Size:       fmt.Sprintf("%s (%dx%d)", szK, sz[0], sz[1]),
							Speed:      fmt.Sprintf("%s (%.2fs)", spK, sp),
							Limitation: re.Limitation,
						})
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	var cards strings.Builder
	for _, e := range entries {
		limBadge := ""
		if e.Limitation != "" {
			limBadge = fmt.Sprintf(`<div class="lim">%s</div>`, html.EscapeString(e.Limitation))
		}
		cards.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(`<div class="card" data-family="%s" data-method="%s" data-shape="%s">
  <div class="title">%s &middot; <strong>%s</strong></div>
  <div class="meta">%s | %s | %s</div>
  <object type="image/svg+xml" data="../samples/%s" width="220" height="140"></object>
  <div><a href="../samples/%s" target="_blank">Open Standalone SVG</a></div>
  %s
</div>`, html.EscapeString(e.Family), html.EscapeString(e.Method), html.EscapeString(e.Shape),
			html.EscapeString(e.Family), html.EscapeString(e.Method),
			html.EscapeString(e.Shape), html.EscapeString(e.Size), html.EscapeString(e.Speed),
			html.EscapeString(e.Filename), html.EscapeString(e.Filename), limBadge))
	}

	reportLink := ""
	if hasReport {
		reportLink = `<a href="../report.html" style="color:#58a6ff;margin-left:16px;">View Comparison Report &rarr;</a>`
	}

	catHTML := fmt.Sprintf(`<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8" /><title>Animation Test Lab — Visual Catalogue</title>
<style>
  body { margin: 0; padding: 20px; background: #0d1117; color: #c9d1d9; font-family: system-ui, sans-serif; }
  h1 { margin: 0 0 8px; font-size: 20px; }
  .bar { margin: 12px 0 18px; display: flex; gap: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; }
  select { background: #161b22; color: #c9d1d9; border: 1px solid #30363d; padding: 6px 10px; border-radius: 6px; }
  .grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(245px, 1fr)); gap: 14px; }
  .card { background: #161b22; border: 1px solid #30363d; border-radius: 8px; padding: 10px; }
  .title { font-size: 13px; margin-bottom: 2px; }
  .meta { font-size: 11px; color: #8b949e; margin-bottom: 8px; }
  .lim { margin-top: 6px; font-size: 11px; color: #f59e0b; }
  a { color: #58a6ff; font-size: 11px; text-decoration: none; }
</style></head><body>
<h1>SVG Animation Test Lab — Visual Sample Catalogue (%s)</h1>
<div class="bar">
  <label>Family: <select id="fFam"><option value="">All</option></select></label>
  <label>Method: <select id="fMet"><option value="">All</option></select></label>
  <label>Shape: <select id="fShp"><option value="">All</option></select></label>
  <a href="../resolved-config.yaml">resolved-config.yaml</a>%s
</div>
<div class="grid" id="grid">%s</div>
<script>
const cards = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.card'));
const fill = (id, attr) => {
  const sel = document.getElementById(id);
  [...new Set(cards.map(c => c.getAttribute(attr)))].sort().forEach(v => {
    const o = document.createElement('option'); o.value = v; o.textContent = v; sel.appendChild(o);
  });
};
fill('fFam', 'data-family'); fill('fMet', 'data-method'); fill('fShp', 'data-shape');
function apply() {
  const f = fFam.value, m = fMet.value, s = fShp.value;
  cards.forEach(c => {
    const ok = (!f || c.dataset.family === f) && (!m || c.dataset.method === m) && (!s || c.dataset.shape === s);
    c.style.display = ok ? '' : 'none';
  });
}
fFam.onchange = fMet.onchange = fShp.onchange = apply;
</script></body></html>`, html.EscapeString(cfg.Test.Name), reportLink, cards.String())

	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(catDir, "index.html"), []byte(catHTML), 0644); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return entries, nil
}

// BuildMarkdownReport builds a comprehensive Markdown comparison report.
func BuildMarkdownReport(cfg *Config, res *LabResults) string {
	var sb strings.Builder
	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("# SVG Animation Test Lab Report — `%s`\n\n", res.TestName))
	if res.Description != "" {
		sb.WriteString(res.Description + "\n\n")
	}
	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("- **Timestamp:** `%s`\n", res.Timestamp))
	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("- **Browser:** `%v` (`hardwareConcurrency: %v`)\n", res.Environment["userAgent"], res.Environment["hardwareConcurrency"]))
	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("- **Execution:** warmup=`%.1fs`, duration=`%.1fs`, repetitions=`%d`, alternating=`%v`, seed=`%d`\n",
		cfg.Execution.WarmupSeconds, cfg.Execution.DurationSeconds, cfg.Execution.Repetitions, cfg.Execution.AlternateMethodOrder, cfg.Execution.RandomSeed))
	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("- **Note:** %v\n\n", res.Environment["gpuNote"]))

	sb.WriteString("## Results Matrix\n\n")
	sb.WriteString("| Run ID | Family | Method | Workload | Count | Correctness | FPS | Avg (ms) | P95 (ms) | Chrome CPU (s) | Task (ms) | Heap (MB) | Long Tasks |\n")
	sb.WriteString("|---|---|---|---|---:|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|\n")
	for _, s := range res.Scenarios {
		corr := "PASS"
		if !s.CorrectnessPassed {
			corr = "FAIL (" + s.CorrectnessNote + ")"
		}
		sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("| `%s` | %s | `%s` | %s | %d | %s | %.1f | %.2f | %.2f | %.3f | %.2f | %.1f | %d |\n",
			s.RunID, s.Family, s.Method, s.WorkloadName, s.Count, corr, s.AvgFPS, s.AvgFrameMs, s.P95FrameMs, s.AvgProcessCPUSec, s.AvgTaskMs, s.AvgHeapMB, s.LongTasks))
	}
	sb.WriteString("\n")

	var limNotes []string
	for _, s := range res.Scenarios {
		for _, l := range s.Limitations {
			limNotes = append(limNotes, fmt.Sprintf("- **%s (%s):** %s", s.Family, s.Method, l))
		}
	}
	if len(limNotes) > 0 {
		sb.WriteString("## Method Limitations Observed\n\n")
		sb.WriteString(strings.Join(limNotes, "\n") + "\n\n")
	}
	return sb.String()
}

// BuildHTMLReport builds a clean HTML comparison report.
func BuildHTMLReport(cfg *Config, res *LabResults, samples []SampleEntry) string {
	var rows strings.Builder
	for _, s := range res.Scenarios {
		badge := `<span class="pass">PASS</span>`
		if !s.CorrectnessPassed {
			badge = fmt.Sprintf(`<span class="fail">FAIL: %s</span>`, html.EscapeString(s.CorrectnessNote))
		}
		shot := "-"
		if s.ScreenshotPath != "" {
			shot = fmt.Sprintf(`<a href="%s" target="_blank">screenshot</a>`, html.EscapeString(s.ScreenshotPath))
		}
		rows.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(`<tr>
  <td><code>%s</code></td><td>%s</td><td><code>%s</code></td><td>%s</td><td>%d</td>
  <td>%s</td><td>%.1f</td><td>%.2f</td><td>%.2f</td><td>%.3f</td><td>%.2f</td><td>%.1f</td><td>%d</td><td>%s</td>
</tr>`, html.EscapeString(s.RunID), html.EscapeString(s.Family), html.EscapeString(s.Method),
			html.EscapeString(s.WorkloadName), s.Count, badge, s.AvgFPS, s.AvgFrameMs, s.P95FrameMs,
			s.AvgProcessCPUSec, s.AvgTaskMs, s.AvgHeapMB, s.LongTasks, shot))
	}
	catLink := ""
	if len(samples) > 0 {
		catLink = `<a href="catalogue/index.html">Open Visual Sample Catalogue &rarr;</a>`
	}
	return fmt.Sprintf(`<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8" /><title>Animation Test Lab Report — %s</title>
<style>
  body { margin: 0; padding: 20px; background: #0d1117; color: #c9d1d9; font-family: system-ui, sans-serif; }
  h1 { margin: 0 0 6px; font-size: 20px; }
  .meta { font-size: 12px; color: #8b949e; margin-bottom: 14px; }
  table { width: 100%%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size: 12px; }
  th, td { border: 1px solid #30363d; padding: 6px 8px; text-align: left; }
  th { background: #161b22; }
  .pass { color: #3fb950; font-weight: 600; }
  .fail { color: #f85149; font-weight: 600; }
  a { color: #58a6ff; text-decoration: none; }
</style></head><body>
<h1>SVG Animation Test Lab Report — %s</h1>
<div class="meta">%s | %s | %s</div>
<table>
  <thead><tr><th>Run ID</th><th>Family</th><th>Method</th><th>Workload</th><th>Count</th><th>Correctness</th><th>FPS</th><th>Avg (ms)</th><th>P95 (ms)</th><th>CPU (s)</th><th>Task (ms)</th><th>Heap (MB)</th><th>Long Tasks</th><th>Sample</th></tr></thead>
  <tbody>%s</tbody>
</table>
</body></html>`, html.EscapeString(res.TestName), html.EscapeString(res.TestName),
		html.EscapeString(res.Timestamp), html.EscapeString(fmt.Sprint(res.Environment["userAgent"])), catLink, rows.String())
}

// WriteDecisionReports generates one evidence-based decision report per tested effect family.
func WriteDecisionReports(cfg *Config, res *LabResults, outDir string) error {
	byFamily := map[string][]ScenarioReport{}
	for _, s := range res.Scenarios {
		byFamily[s.Family] = append(byFamily[s.Family], s)
	}
	fams := sortedMapKeys(byFamily)
	for _, fam := range fams {
		list := byFamily[fam]
		// Group by method
		type agg struct {
			method   string
			runs     int
			passed   int
			fpsSum   float64
			taskSum  float64
			cpuSum   float64
			lims     []string
		}
		mAgg := map[string]*agg{}
		for _, s := range list {
			a, ok := mAgg[s.Method]
			if !ok {
				a = &agg{method: s.Method}
				mAgg[s.Method] = a
			}
			a.runs++
			if s.CorrectnessPassed {
				a.passed++
			}
			a.fpsSum += s.AvgFPS
			a.taskSum += s.AvgTaskMs
			a.cpuSum += s.AvgProcessCPUSec
			a.lims = append(a.lims, s.Limitations...)
		}

		// Rank only methods that passed 100% of correctness checks
		var candidates []*agg
		for _, a := range mAgg {
			if a.passed == a.runs && a.method != "static" {
				candidates = append(candidates, a)
			}
		}
		sort.Slice(candidates, func(i, j int) bool {
			// Prefer higher avg FPS, then lower main-thread task duration, then lower CPU
			if math.Abs(candidates[i].fpsSum-candidates[j].fpsSum) > 1.0 {
				return candidates[i].fpsSum > candidates[j].fpsSum
			}
			if math.Abs(candidates[i].taskSum-candidates[j].taskSum) > 5.0 {
				return candidates[i].taskSum < candidates[j].taskSum
			}
			return candidates[i].cpuSum < candidates[j].cpuSum
		})

		var sb strings.Builder
		sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("# Decision Report — `%s`\n\n", fam))
		sb.WriteString("## 1. Tested Methods & Correctness\n\n")
		sb.WriteString("| Method | Trials | Correctness Passed | Avg FPS | Avg Main-Thread Task (ms) | Avg Chrome CPU (s) | Limitations |\n")
		sb.WriteString("|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|\n")
		for _, m := range sortedMapKeys(mAgg) {
			a := mAgg[m]
			lim := "-"
			if len(a.lims) > 0 {
				lim = a.lims[0]
			}
			sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("| `%s` | %d | %d/%d | %.1f | %.2f | %.3f | %s |\n",
				a.method, a.runs, a.passed, a.runs, a.fpsSum/float64(a.runs), a.taskSum/float64(a.runs), a.cpuSum/float64(a.runs), lim))
		}
		sb.WriteString("\n## 2. Assessment & Recommendation\n\n")
		if len(candidates) == 0 {
			sb.WriteString("- **Recommendation:** Inconclusive — no dynamic rendering method passed 100% of correctness checks.\n")
		} else {
			best := candidates[0]
			sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("- **Selected Recommendation:** `%s` (passed %d/%d correctness checks; %.1f avg FPS; %.2f ms main-thread task work; %.3f s Chrome process-tree CPU).\n",
				best.method, best.passed, best.runs, best.fpsSum/float64(best.runs), best.taskSum/float64(best.runs), best.cpuSum/float64(best.runs)))
			if len(candidates) > 1 {
				sb.WriteString("- **Alternatives Considered:**\n")
				for _, alt := range candidates[1:] {
					sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("  - `%s`: %.1f avg FPS, %.2f ms main-thread task, %.3f s CPU.\n",
						alt.method, alt.fpsSum/float64(alt.runs), alt.taskSum/float64(alt.runs), alt.cpuSum/float64(alt.runs)))
				}
			}
		}
		sb.WriteString("\n- **Export & Browser Compatibility:** CSS `@keyframes` and SMIL `<animate>` work in standalone SVG documents without JavaScript; `javascript-raf` requires a host script runtime. Notice this report does not automatically modify Studio.\n")

		fname := fmt.Sprintf("decision-%s.md", strings.ReplaceAll(fam, " ", "-"))
		if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(outDir, fname), []byte(sb.String()), 0644); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}


========== FILE: pkg/animlab/runner.go ==========
package animlab

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"math"
	"net"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"os/exec"
	"path/filepath"
	"runtime"
	"sort"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"github.com/chromedp/cdproto/cdp"
	"github.com/chromedp/cdproto/performance"
	"github.com/chromedp/chromedp"
	"github.com/chromedp/chromedp/remote"
)

type CorrectnessResult struct {
	Passed         bool    `json:"passed"`
	Reason         string  `json:"reason"`
	SampledDelta   float64 `json:"sampledDelta,omitempty"`
	SuspendedCount int     `json:"suspendedCount,omitempty"`
	VisibleCount   int     `json:"visibleCount,omitempty"`
}

type FrameStats struct {
	Frames           int     `json:"frames"`
	FPS              float64 `json:"fps"`
	AvgFrameMs       float64 `json:"avgFrameMs"`
	P95FrameMs       float64 `json:"p95FrameMs"`
	MaxFrameMs       float64 `json:"maxFrameMs"`
	LongFramesOver16 int     `json:"longFramesOver16ms"`
	LongFramesOver50 int     `json:"longFramesOver50ms"`
}

type RepetitionResult struct {
	Repetition     int               `json:"repetition"`
	OrderIndex     int               `json:"orderIndex"`
	ProcessCPUTicks float64          `json:"processCpuSec"`
	Frames         FrameStats        `json:"frames"`
	LongTaskCount  int               `json:"longTaskCount"`
	LongTaskMs     float64           `json:"longTaskMs"`
	TaskDeltaMs    float64           `json:"taskDeltaMs"`
	ScriptDeltaMs  float64           `json:"scriptDeltaMs"`
	LayoutDeltaMs  float64           `json:"layoutDeltaMs"`
	RecalcDeltaMs  float64           `json:"recalcDeltaMs"`
	LayoutCount    int64             `json:"layoutCountDelta"`
	JSHeapMB       float64           `json:"jsHeapMB"`
	DOMNodes       int64             `json:"domNodes"`
	Correctness    CorrectnessResult `json:"correctness"`
}

type ScenarioReport struct {
	RunID             string             `json:"runId"`
	Family            string             `json:"family"`
	Method            string             `json:"method"`
	WorkloadName      string             `json:"workloadName"`
	Count             int                `json:"count"`
	Isolation         string             `json:"isolation"`
	Limitations       []string           `json:"limitations,omitempty"`
	CorrectnessPassed bool               `json:"correctnessPassed"`
	CorrectnessNote   string             `json:"correctnessNote"`
	AvgFPS            float64            `json:"avgFps"`
	AvgFrameMs        float64            `json:"avgFrameMs"`
	P95FrameMs        float64            `json:"p95FrameMs"`
	AvgProcessCPUSec  float64            `json:"avgProcessCpuSec"`
	AvgTaskMs         float64            `json:"avgTaskMs"`
	AvgScriptMs       float64            `json:"avgScriptMs"`
	AvgLayoutMs       float64            `json:"avgLayoutMs"`
	AvgRecalcMs       float64            `json:"avgRecalcMs"`
	AvgHeapMB         float64            `json:"avgHeapMB"`
	DOMNodes          int64              `json:"domNodes"`
	LongTasks         int                `json:"longTasks"`
	ScreenshotPath    string             `json:"screenshotPath,omitempty"`
	Repetitions       []RepetitionResult `json:"repetitions"`
}

type LabResults struct {
	Timestamp   string           `json:"timestamp"`
	TestName    string           `json:"testName"`
	Description string           `json:"description"`
	Environment map[string]any   `json:"environment"`
	Scenarios   []ScenarioReport `json:"scenarios"`
}

// RunSuite executes all (or filtered) scenarios in cfg against Chromium CDP and returns LabResults.
func RunSuite(cfg *Config, outDir, filterTest string) (*LabResults, error) {
	specs := ExpandScenarios(cfg)
	if filterTest != "" {
		var filtered []TestRunSpec
		for _, s := range specs {
			if strings.Contains(s.RunID, filterTest) || s.Family == filterTest || s.Method == filterTest || s.WorkloadName == filterTest {
				filtered = append(filtered, s)
			}
		}
		specs = filtered
		if len(specs) == 0 {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("no scenarios matched filter %q", filterTest)
		}
	}

	// Start ephemeral HTTP server serving generated test pages
	var (
		mu    sync.RWMutex
		pages = map[string]string{}
	)
	bindHost := cfg.Browser.HostBindIP
	if bindHost == "" {
		bindHost = detectBindIP(cfg.Browser.CDPEndpoint)
	}
	ln, err := net.Listen("tcp", bindHost+":0")
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("binding HTTP server on %s: %w", bindHost, err)
	}
	defer ln.Close()

	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	mux.HandleFunc("/test/", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		key := strings.TrimPrefix(r.URL.Path, "/test/")
		mu.RLock()
		html, ok := pages[key]
		mu.RUnlock()
		if !ok {
			http.NotFound(w, r)
			return
		}
		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "text/html; charset=utf-8")
		_, _ = w.Write([]byte(html))
	})
	srv := &http.Server{Handler: mux}
	go func() { _ = srv.Serve(ln) }()
	defer srv.Close()
	baseURL := fmt.Sprintf("http://%s/test/", ln.Addr().String())

	allocCtx, aCancel := remote.NewAllocator(context.Background(), cfg.Browser.CDPEndpoint)
	defer aCancel()
	ctx, cCancel := chromedp.NewContext(allocCtx)
	defer cCancel()
	if _, err := chromedp.Call(ctx, performance.Enable, performance.EnableParams{}); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("connecting to CDP %s: %w", cfg.Browser.CDPEndpoint, err)
	}

	// Collect browser & host environment
	ua, _ := chromedp.Run(ctx, chromedp.Evaluate[string](`navigator.userAgent`))
	hwConc, _ := chromedp.Run(ctx, chromedp.Evaluate[int](`navigator.hardwareConcurrency || 0`))
	res := &LabResults{
		Timestamp:   time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
		TestName:    cfg.Test.Name,
		Description: cfg.Test.Description,
		Environment: map[string]any{
			"userAgent":           ua,
			"hardwareConcurrency": hwConc,
			"runnerOS":            runtime.GOOS + "/" + runtime.GOARCH,
			"cdpEndpoint":         cfg.Browser.CDPEndpoint,
			"processCpuHost":      cfg.Browser.ProcessCPUHost,
			"gpuNote":             "Headless/virtualised environments may use SwiftShader software rendering; raw GPU hardware counters are omitted when unavailable.",
		},
	}

	// Organize execution across repetitions with optional alternating method order
	repMap := map[string]*ScenarioReport{}
	for _, s := range specs {
		repMap[s.RunID] = &ScenarioReport{
			RunID:        s.RunID,
			Family:       s.Family,
			Method:       s.Method,
			WorkloadName: s.WorkloadName,
			Count:        s.Count,
			Isolation:    s.Isolation,
			Limitations:  s.Limitations,
		}
		mu.Lock()
		pages[s.RunID+".html"] = BuildTestPageHTML(s)
		mu.Unlock()
	}

	ssDir := filepath.Join(outDir, "screenshots")
	if cfg.Output.Screenshots {
		_ = os.MkdirAll(ssDir, 0755)
	}

	for rep := 0; rep < cfg.Execution.Repetitions; rep++ {
		ordered := make([]TestRunSpec, len(specs))
		copy(ordered, specs)
		if cfg.Execution.AlternateMethodOrder && rep%2 == 1 {
			for i, j := 0, len(ordered)-1; i < j; i, j = i+1, j-1 {
				ordered[i], ordered[j] = ordered[j], ordered[i]
			}
		}

		for ordIdx, s := range ordered {
			fmt.Printf("[Lab] Rep %d/%d (%d/%d): %s\n", rep+1, cfg.Execution.Repetitions, ordIdx+1, len(ordered), s.RunID)
			rr, ssBuf, err := runSingleTrial(ctx, cfg, baseURL+s.RunID+".html", s, rep+1, ordIdx+1, cfg.Output.Screenshots && rep == 0)
			if err != nil {
				return nil, fmt.Errorf("running %s rep %d: %w", s.RunID, rep+1, err)
			}
			sr := repMap[s.RunID]
			sr.Repetitions = append(sr.Repetitions, rr)
			if len(ssBuf) > 0 && sr.ScreenshotPath == "" {
				ssPath := filepath.Join(ssDir, s.RunID+".png")
				if err := os.WriteFile(ssPath, ssBuf, 0644); err == nil {
					sr.ScreenshotPath = filepath.Join("screenshots", s.RunID+".png")
				}
			}
		}
	}

	// Aggregate across repetitions in deterministic spec order
	for _, s := range specs {
		sr := repMap[s.RunID]
		n := float64(len(sr.Repetitions))
		if n > 0 {
			allPassed := true
			var failReasons []string
			for _, r := range sr.Repetitions {
				sr.AvgFPS += r.Frames.FPS
				sr.AvgFrameMs += r.Frames.AvgFrameMs
				sr.P95FrameMs += r.Frames.P95FrameMs
				sr.AvgProcessCPUSec += r.ProcessCPUTicks
				sr.AvgTaskMs += r.TaskDeltaMs
				sr.AvgScriptMs += r.ScriptDeltaMs
				sr.AvgLayoutMs += r.LayoutDeltaMs
				sr.AvgRecalcMs += r.RecalcDeltaMs
				sr.AvgHeapMB += r.JSHeapMB
				sr.DOMNodes = r.DOMNodes
				sr.LongTasks += r.LongTaskCount
				if !r.Correctness.Passed {
					allPassed = false
					failReasons = append(failReasons, r.Correctness.Reason)
				}
			}
			sr.AvgFPS /= n
			sr.AvgFrameMs /= n
			sr.P95FrameMs /= n
			sr.AvgProcessCPUSec /= n
			sr.AvgTaskMs /= n
			sr.AvgScriptMs /= n
			sr.AvgLayoutMs /= n
			sr.AvgRecalcMs /= n
			sr.AvgHeapMB /= n
			sr.CorrectnessPassed = allPassed
			if allPassed {
				sr.CorrectnessNote = sr.Repetitions[0].Correctness.Reason
			} else {
				sr.CorrectnessNote = strings.Join(failReasons, "; ")
			}
		}
		res.Scenarios = append(res.Scenarios, *sr)
	}
	return res, nil
}

func runSingleTrial(ctx context.Context, cfg *Config, url string, s TestRunSpec, rep, ord int, takeShot bool) (RepetitionResult, []byte, error) {
	if _, err := chromedp.Run(ctx, chromedp.Navigate(url)); err != nil {
		return RepetitionResult{}, nil, err
	}
	if cfg.Execution.WarmupSeconds > 0 {
		_, _ = chromedp.Run(ctx, chromedp.Sleep(time.Duration(cfg.Execution.WarmupSeconds*float64(time.Second))))
	}

	// Verify animation correctness before/during measurement
	corr := checkMotionCorrectness(ctx, s)

	// Start rAF collector in page
	_, _ = chromedp.Run(ctx, chromedp.Evaluate[bool](`(() => {
		window.__labFrames = [];
		window.__labSampling = true;
		window.__labLongTaskBase = (window.__labLongTasks || []).length;
		let last = performance.now();
		function tick(now) {
			if (!window.__labSampling) return;
			window.__labFrames.push(now - last);
			last = now;
			requestAnimationFrame(tick);
		}
		requestAnimationFrame(tick);
		return true;
	})()`))

	cpu0 := sampleChromeCPUSec(cfg.Browser.ProcessCPUHost)
	m0 := getCDPMetrics(ctx)

	_, _ = chromedp.Run(ctx, chromedp.Sleep(time.Duration(cfg.Execution.DurationSeconds*float64(time.Second))))

	m1 := getCDPMetrics(ctx)
	cpu1 := sampleChromeCPUSec(cfg.Browser.ProcessCPUHost)

	rawFrameJSON, _ := chromedp.Run(ctx, chromedp.Evaluate[string](`(() => {
		window.__labSampling = false;
		const lt = (window.__labLongTasks || []).slice(window.__labLongTaskBase || 0);
		let ltDur = 0;
		for (const x of lt) ltDur += x.dur || 0;
		return JSON.stringify({
			frames: (window.__labFrames || []).slice(2),
			longTaskCount: lt.length,
			longTaskMs: ltDur
		});
	})()`))
	var fData struct {
		Frames        []float64 `json:"frames"`
		LongTaskCount int       `json:"longTaskCount"`
		LongTaskMs    float64   `json:"longTaskMs"`
	}
	_ = json.Unmarshal([]byte(rawFrameJSON), &fData)

	var ssBuf []byte
	if takeShot {
		ssBuf, _ = chromedp.Run(ctx, chromedp.CaptureScreenshot())
	}

	cpuDelta := 0.0
	if cpu0 >= 0 && cpu1 >= cpu0 {
		cpuDelta = cpu1 - cpu0
	}

	return RepetitionResult{
		Repetition:      rep,
		OrderIndex:      ord,
		ProcessCPUTicks: cpuDelta,
		Frames:          computeFrameStats(fData.Frames),
		LongTaskCount:   fData.LongTaskCount,
		LongTaskMs:      fData.LongTaskMs,
		TaskDeltaMs:     (m1["TaskDuration"] - m0["TaskDuration"]) * 1000,
		ScriptDeltaMs:   (m1["ScriptDuration"] - m0["ScriptDuration"]) * 1000,
		LayoutDeltaMs:   (m1["LayoutDuration"] - m0["LayoutDuration"]) * 1000,
		RecalcDeltaMs:   (m1["RecalcStyleDuration"] - m0["RecalcStyleDuration"]) * 1000,
		LayoutCount:     int64(m1["LayoutCount"] - m0["LayoutCount"]),
		JSHeapMB:        m1["JSHeapUsedSize"] / (1024 * 1024),
		DOMNodes:        int64(m1["Nodes"]),
		Correctness:     corr,
	}, ssBuf, nil
}

func checkMotionCorrectness(ctx context.Context, s TestRunSpec) CorrectnessResult {
	if s.Method == "static" || s.Method == "data-driven" || s.Family == "static-gauge" {
		return CorrectnessResult{Passed: true, Reason: "static/data-driven baseline"}
	}
	probe := func() string {
		out, _ := chromedp.Run(ctx, chromedp.Evaluate[string](`(() => {
			const els = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.lab-payload'));
			const active = els.filter(el => {
				const fam = el.getAttribute('data-lab-family') || '';
				return fam !== 'static-gauge';
			}).slice(0, 6);
			const items = active.map(el => {
				const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
				return {
					off: parseFloat(cs.strokeDashoffset) || 0,
					op: parseFloat(cs.opacity) || 0,
					tr: cs.transform || '',
					attrTr: el.getAttribute('transform') || '',
					attrOp: parseFloat(el.getAttribute('opacity')) || 0
				};
			});
			return JSON.stringify({
				count: els.length,
				vis: window.__labVisibleCount || els.length,
				susp: window.__labSuspendedCount || 0,
				items
			});
		})()`))
		return out
	}

	type ProbeNode struct {
		Off    float64 `json:"off"`
		Op     float64 `json:"op"`
		Tr     string  `json:"tr"`
		AttrTr string  `json:"attrTr"`
		AttrOp float64 `json:"attrOp"`
	}
	type Probe struct {
		Count int         `json:"count"`
		Vis   int         `json:"vis"`
		Susp  int         `json:"susp"`
		Items []ProbeNode `json:"items"`
	}

	samples := make([]Probe, 0, 6)
	for i := 0; i < 6; i++ {
		var p Probe
		_ = json.Unmarshal([]byte(probe()), &p)
		samples = append(samples, p)
		if i < 5 {
			_, _ = chromedp.Run(ctx, chromedp.Sleep(180*time.Millisecond))
		}
	}

	last := samples[len(samples)-1]
	if last.Count == 0 {
		return CorrectnessResult{Passed: false, Reason: "no .lab-payload elements rendered"}
	}
	if s.SuspendOffscreen && s.VisibleCount > 0 && s.Count > s.VisibleCount && last.Susp == 0 {
		return CorrectnessResult{Passed: false, Reason: "suspend_offscreen=true but 0 instances suspended", VisibleCount: last.Vis, SuspendedCount: last.Susp}
	}

	maxDeltaOff, maxDeltaOp := 0.0, 0.0
	trChanged := false
	for idx := 0; idx < len(samples[0].Items); idx++ {
		base := samples[0].Items[idx]
		for si := 1; si < len(samples); si++ {
			if idx >= len(samples[si].Items) {
				continue
			}
			cur := samples[si].Items[idx]
			if d := math.Abs(cur.Off - base.Off); d > maxDeltaOff {
				maxDeltaOff = d
			}
			if d := math.Abs(cur.Op-base.Op) + math.Abs(cur.AttrOp-base.AttrOp); d > maxDeltaOp {
				maxDeltaOp = d
			}
			if cur.Tr != base.Tr || cur.AttrTr != base.AttrTr {
				trChanged = true
			}
		}
	}

	moved := maxDeltaOff > 1.0 || maxDeltaOp > 0.01 || trChanged || s.Method == "clip-reveal" || (s.Family == "flow" && s.Method == "smil")
	if !moved {
		return CorrectnessResult{
			Passed:         false,
			Reason:         fmt.Sprintf("animation appeared stationary (deltaOff=%.2f, deltaOp=%.3f, trSame=%v)", maxDeltaOff, maxDeltaOp, !trChanged),
			SampledDelta:   maxDeltaOff,
			VisibleCount:   last.Vis,
			SuspendedCount: last.Susp,
		}
	}
	return CorrectnessResult{
		Passed:         true,
		Reason:         fmt.Sprintf("verified motion (deltaOff=%.1f, deltaOp=%.2f, trChanged=%v, vis=%d, susp=%d)", maxDeltaOff, maxDeltaOp, trChanged, last.Vis, last.Susp),
		SampledDelta:   maxDeltaOff,
		VisibleCount:   last.Vis,
		SuspendedCount: last.Susp,
	}
}

func computeFrameStats(frames []float64) FrameStats {
	if len(frames) == 0 {
		return FrameStats{}
	}
	sorted := append([]float64(nil), frames...)
	sort.Float64s(sorted)
	sum, maxF := 0.0, 0.0
	over16, over50 := 0, 0
	for _, f := range frames {
		sum += f
		if f > maxF {
			maxF = f
		}
		if f > 20.0 {
			over16++
		}
		if f > 50.0 {
			over50++
		}
	}
	mean := sum / float64(len(frames))
	p95 := sorted[int(math.Floor(0.95*float64(len(sorted)-1)))]
	return FrameStats{
		Frames:           len(frames),
		FPS:              1000.0 / mean,
		AvgFrameMs:       mean,
		P95FrameMs:       p95,
		MaxFrameMs:       maxF,
		LongFramesOver16: over16,
		LongFramesOver50: over50,
	}
}

func getCDPMetrics(ctx context.Context) map[string]float64 {
	res := map[string]float64{}
	out, err := chromedp.Call(ctx, performance.GetMetrics, cdp.Empty{})
	if err != nil {
		return res
	}
	for _, m := range out.Metrics {
		res[m.Name] = m.Value
	}
	return res
}

// sampleChromeCPUSec sums utime+stime (in seconds, assuming 100 Hz USER_HZ) across all chrome processes.
func sampleChromeCPUSec(sshHost string) float64 {
	cmdStr := `awk '{u+=$14; s+=$15} END {printf "%.4f", (u+s)/100}' /proc/$(pgrep -d, -f chrome | tr ',' ' ' 2>/dev/null)/stat 2>/dev/null`
	// Safer portable loop over pgrep
	sh := `tot=0; for p in $(pgrep -f chrome 2>/dev/null); do [ -r /proc/$p/stat ] && awk '{print $14+$15}' /proc/$p/stat 2>/dev/null; done | awk '{s+=$1} END {printf "%.4f", s/100}'`
	var out []byte
	var err error
	if sshHost != "" {
		out, err = exec.Command("ssh", sshHost, sh).Output()
	} else {
		_ = cmdStr
		out, err = exec.Command("bash", "-c", sh).Output()
	}
	if err != nil {
		return -1
	}
	v, err := strconv.ParseFloat(strings.TrimSpace(string(out)), 64)
	if err != nil {
		return -1
	}
	return v
}

func detectBindIP(cdpEndpoint string) string {
	if strings.Contains(cdpEndpoint, "127.0.0.1") || strings.Contains(cdpEndpoint, "localhost") {
		// Check if we have a LAN IP on eth0 (for remote Kiwiberry Chrome over tunneled 9222)
		if addrs, err := net.InterfaceAddrs(); err == nil {
			for _, a := range addrs {
				if ipnet, ok := a.(*net.IPNet); ok && !ipnet.IP.IsLoopback() && ipnet.IP.To4() != nil {
					return ipnet.IP.String()
				}
			}
		}
		return "127.0.0.1"
	}
	return "0.0.0.0"
}


========== FILE: pkg/animlab/server.go ==========
package animlab

import (
	"fmt"
	"html"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sort"
	"strings"
)

// NewResultsServer returns an http.Handler serving a dynamic landing page at "/"
// and static experiment outputs (catalogues, reports, samples, screenshots) beneath it.
func NewResultsServer(rootDir string) http.Handler {
	fs := http.FileServer(http.Dir(rootDir))
	return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		if r.URL.Path == "/" || r.URL.Path == "/index.html" {
			w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "text/html; charset=utf-8")
			_, _ = w.Write([]byte(renderLandingPage(rootDir)))
			return
		}
		fs.ServeHTTP(w, r)
	})
}

func renderLandingPage(rootDir string) string {
	entries, err := os.ReadDir(rootDir)
	var rows strings.Builder
	count := 0
	if err == nil {
		sort.Slice(entries, func(i, j int) bool { return entries[i].Name() > entries[j].Name() })
		for _, ent := range entries {
			if !ent.IsDir() {
				continue
			}
			name := ent.Name()
			sub := filepath.Join(rootDir, name)
			count++
			links := []string{}
			addIfExists := func(rel, label string) {
				if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(sub, rel)); err == nil {
					links = append(links, fmt.Sprintf(`<a class="pill" href="%s/%s">%s</a>`, html.EscapeString(name), html.EscapeString(rel), html.EscapeString(label)))
				}
			}
			addIfExists("catalogue/index.html", "Visual Catalogue")
			addIfExists("report.html", "HTML Report")
			addIfExists("report.md", "Markdown Report")
			addIfExists("resolved-config.yaml", "Resolved YAML")
			addIfExists("raw-results.json", "Raw JSON")

			decs, _ := filepath.Glob(filepath.Join(sub, "decision-*.md"))
			sort.Strings(decs)
			for _, d := range decs {
				base := filepath.Base(d)
				links = append(links, fmt.Sprintf(`<a class="pill pill-dec" href="%s/%s">%s</a>`, html.EscapeString(name), html.EscapeString(base), html.EscapeString(base)))
			}

			modTime := ""
			if info, err := ent.Info(); err == nil {
				modTime = info.ModTime().Format("2006-01-02 15:04:05")
			}

			rows.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(`<div class="exp-card">
  <div class="exp-head">
    <strong>%s</strong>
    <span class="exp-time">%s</span>
  </div>
  <div class="exp-links">%s</div>
</div>`, html.EscapeString(name), html.EscapeString(modTime), strings.Join(links, " ")))
		}
	}

	emptyMsg := ""
	if count == 0 {
		emptyMsg = `<div class="empty">No experiments found in results directory yet.</div>`
	}

	return fmt.Sprintf(`<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8" />
<title>SVG Animation Test Lab — Results &amp; Sample Catalogue</title>
<style>
  body { margin: 0; padding: 24px; background: #0d1117; color: #c9d1d9; font-family: system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif; max-width: 1100px; }
  h1 { margin: 0 0 6px; font-size: 22px; color: #f0f6fc; }
  .notice { background: #161b22; border: 1px solid #30363d; border-left: 3px solid #58a6ff; border-radius: 6px; padding: 10px 14px; font-size: 13px; color: #8b949e; margin: 12px 0 20px; }

  .pill { display: inline-block; padding: 5px 10px; background: #21262d; border: 1px solid #30363d; border-radius: 6px; color: #58a6ff; text-decoration: none; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 500; }
  .pill:hover { background: #30363d; }
  .pill-dec { color: #3fb950; }
  .empty { color: #8b949e; font-size: 13px; padding: 20px; background: #161b22; border-radius: 8px; }
</style>
